Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in microevolution theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 558 and the invariant I(A,B) = 751 mod 2.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^751(S, F) is trivial. QED.
